User2026-07-03T01:02:13.802Z

Base directory for this skill: C:\Users\kashidashi01.claude\plugins\cache\superpowers-marketplace\superpowers\6.0.3\skills\systematic-debugging

Systematic Debugging

Overview

Random fixes waste time and create new bugs. Quick patches mask underlying issues.

Core principle: ALWAYS find root cause before attempting fixes. Symptom fixes are failure.

Violating the letter of this process is violating the spirit of debugging.

The Iron Law

NO FIXES WITHOUT ROOT CAUSE INVESTIGATION FIRST

If you haven't completed Phase 1, you cannot propose fixes.

When to Use

Use for ANY technical issue: - Test failures - Bugs in production - Unexpected behavior - Performance problems - Build failures - Integration issues

Use this ESPECIALLY when: - Under time pressure (emergencies make guessing tempting) - "Just one quick fix" seems obvious - You've already tried multiple fixes - Previous fix didn't work - You don't fully understand the issue

Don't skip when: - Issue seems simple (simple bugs have root causes too) - You're in a hurry (rushing guarantees rework) - Manager wants it fixed NOW (systematic is faster than thrashing)

The Four Phases

You MUST complete each phase before proceeding to the next.

Phase 1: Root Cause Investigation

BEFORE attempting ANY fix:

  1. Read Error Messages Carefully
  2. Don't skip past errors or warnings
  3. They often contain the exact solution
  4. Read stack traces completely

  5. Note line numbers, file paths, error codes

  6. Reproduce Consistently

  7. Can you trigger it reliably?
  8. What are the exact steps?
  9. Does it happen every time?

  10. If not reproducible → gather more data, don't guess

  11. Check Recent Changes

  12. What changed that could cause this?
  13. Git diff, recent commits
  14. New dependencies, config changes

  15. Environmental differences

  16. Gather Evidence in Multi-Component Systems

WHEN system has multiple components (CI → build → signing, API → service → database):

BEFORE proposing fixes, add diagnostic instrumentation: ``` For EACH component boundary: - Log what data enters component - Log what data exits component - Verify environment/config propagation - Check state at each layer

Run once to gather evidence showing WHERE it breaks THEN analyze evidence to identify failing component THEN investigate that specific component ```

Example (multi-layer system): ```bash # Layer 1: Workflow echo "=== Secrets available in workflow: ===" echo "IDENTITY: ${IDENTITY:+SET}${IDENTITY:-UNSET}"

# Layer 2: Build script echo "=== Env vars in build script: ===" env | grep IDENTITY || echo "IDENTITY not in environment"

# Layer 3: Signing script echo "=== Keychain state: ===" security list-keychains security find-identity -v

# Layer 4: Actual signing codesign --sign "$IDENTITY" --verbose=4 "$APP" ```

This reveals: Which layer fails (secrets → workflow ✓, workflow → build ✗)

  1. Trace Data Flow

WHEN error is deep in call stack:

See root-cause-tracing.md in this directory for the complete backward tracing technique.

Quick version: - Where does bad value originate? - What called this with bad value? - Keep tracing up until you find the source - Fix at source, not at symptom

Phase 2: Pattern Analysis

Find the pattern before fixing:

  1. Find Working Examples
  2. Locate similar working code in same codebase

  3. What works that's similar to what's broken?

  4. Compare Against References

  5. If implementing pattern, read reference implementation COMPLETELY
  6. Don't skim - read every line

  7. Understand the pattern fully before applying

  8. Identify Differences

  9. What's different between working and broken?
  10. List every difference, however small

  11. Don't assume "that can't matter"

  12. Understand Dependencies

  13. What other components does this need?
  14. What settings, config, environment?
  15. What assumptions does it make?

Phase 3: Hypothesis and Testing

Scientific method:

  1. Form Single Hypothesis
  2. State clearly: "I think X is the root cause because Y"
  3. Write it down

  4. Be specific, not vague

  5. Test Minimally

  6. Make the SMALLEST possible change to test hypothesis
  7. One variable at a time

  8. Don't fix multiple things at once

  9. Verify Before Continuing

  10. Did it work? Yes → Phase 4
  11. Didn't work? Form NEW hypothesis

  12. DON'T add more fixes on top

  13. When You Don't Know

  14. Say "I don't understand X"
  15. Don't pretend to know
  16. Ask for help
  17. Research more

Phase 4: Implementation

Fix the root cause, not the symptom:

  1. Create Failing Test Case
  2. Simplest possible reproduction
  3. Automated test if possible
  4. One-off test script if no framework
  5. MUST have before fixing

  6. Use the superpowers:test-driven-development skill for writing proper failing tests

  7. Implement Single Fix

  8. Address the root cause identified
  9. ONE change at a time
  10. No "while I'm here" improvements

  11. No bundled refactoring

  12. Verify Fix

  13. Test passes now?
  14. No other tests broken?

  15. Issue actually resolved?

  16. If Fix Doesn't Work

  17. STOP
  18. Count: How many fixes have you tried?
  19. If < 3: Return to Phase 1, re-analyze with new information
  20. If ≥ 3: STOP and question the architecture (step 5 below)

  21. DON'T attempt Fix #4 without architectural discussion

  22. If 3+ Fixes Failed: Question Architecture

Pattern indicating architectural problem: - Each fix reveals new shared state/coupling/problem in different place - Fixes require "massive refactoring" to implement - Each fix creates new symptoms elsewhere

STOP and question fundamentals: - Is this pattern fundamentally sound? - Are we "sticking with it through sheer inertia"? - Should we refactor architecture vs. continue fixing symptoms?

Discuss with your human partner before attempting more fixes

This is NOT a failed hypothesis - this is a wrong architecture.

Red Flags - STOP and Follow Process

If you catch yourself thinking: - "Quick fix for now, investigate later" - "Just try changing X and see if it works" - "Add multiple changes, run tests" - "Skip the test, I'll manually verify" - "It's probably X, let me fix that" - "I don't fully understand but this might work" - "Pattern says X but I'll adapt it differently" - "Here are the main problems: [lists fixes without investigation]" - Proposing solutions before tracing data flow - "One more fix attempt" (when already tried 2+) - Each fix reveals new problem in different place

ALL of these mean: STOP. Return to Phase 1.

If 3+ fixes failed: Question the architecture (see Phase 4.5)

your human partner's Signals You're Doing It Wrong

Watch for these redirections: - "Is that not happening?" - You assumed without verifying - "Will it show us...?" - You should have added evidence gathering - "Stop guessing" - You're proposing fixes without understanding - "Ultra-think this" - Question fundamentals, not just symptoms - "We're stuck?" (frustrated) - Your approach isn't working

When you see these: STOP. Return to Phase 1.

Common Rationalizations

Excuse Reality
"Issue is simple, don't need process" Simple issues have root causes too. Process is fast for simple bugs.
"Emergency, no time for process" Systematic debugging is FASTER than guess-and-check thrashing.
"Just try this first, then investigate" First fix sets the pattern. Do it right from the start.
"I'll write test after confirming fix works" Untested fixes don't stick. Test first proves it.
"Multiple fixes at once saves time" Can't isolate what worked. Causes new bugs.
"Reference too long, I'll adapt the pattern" Partial understanding guarantees bugs. Read it completely.
"I see the problem, let me fix it" Seeing symptoms ≠ understanding root cause.
"One more fix attempt" (after 2+ failures) 3+ failures = architectural problem. Question pattern, don't fix again.

Quick Reference

Phase Key Activities Success Criteria
1. Root Cause Read errors, reproduce, check changes, gather evidence Understand WHAT and WHY
2. Pattern Find working examples, compare Identify differences
3. Hypothesis Form theory, test minimally Confirmed or new hypothesis
4. Implementation Create test, fix, verify Bug resolved, tests pass

When Process Reveals "No Root Cause"

If systematic investigation reveals issue is truly environmental, timing-dependent, or external:

  1. You've completed the process
  2. Document what you investigated
  3. Implement appropriate handling (retry, timeout, error message)
  4. Add monitoring/logging for future investigation

But: 95% of "no root cause" cases are incomplete investigation.

Supporting Techniques

These techniques are part of systematic debugging and available in this directory:

  • root-cause-tracing.md - Trace bugs backward through call stack to find original trigger
  • defense-in-depth.md - Add validation at multiple layers after finding root cause
  • condition-based-waiting.md - Replace arbitrary timeouts with condition polling

Related skills: - superpowers:test-driven-development - For creating failing test case (Phase 4, Step 1) - superpowers:verification-before-completion - Verify fix worked before claiming success

Real-World Impact

From debugging sessions: - Systematic approach: 15-30 minutes to fix - Random fixes approach: 2-3 hours of thrashing - First-time fix rate: 95% vs 40% - New bugs introduced: Near zero vs common

344	 <div class=journal-content>
345	 <div id=journal-37699-notes class="wiki journal-note" data-quote-reply-target=content><p>■問題分析<br>登録機3303から会計機3404へ送信<br>会計機側復元開始から、登録機は中間取引状態チェックのループ処理を起動します<br>今回復元処理で時間がかかるので、登録機側送信失敗の状態になりました<br>登録機側「再実行」押下すると、<br>登録機の状態が「SalesTran_WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction」になりました。<br>その時に会計機から復元完了の通知が来て、上記ループ処理を停止し、送信完了コマンド「Sales_NotifyCustomer」を実行し<br>しかし、状態が変わりましたので、コマンド実行できなかった。<br>画面は再送信中の画面のままで固まった。</p>
346	 <p>■再現性<br>実機で再現できていません<br>ＶＭ環境でデバッグで再現できます。</p>
347	 <p>■対策<br>プログラム改修対応しなければなりません<br>案１）中間取引状態チェックのループ処理の起動と停止　見直し<br>　中間取引復元開始から 　ループ処理起動<br>　再送信状態になるから 　ループ処理停止<br>　送信完了通知が来るから　ループ処理停止<br>　再送信ボタン押下後　　　ループ処理起動<br>案２）再送信の状態で、送信完了コマンドを実行できるように<br>　StateWinPOSSales.xml編集<br>　※固まる問題を解消できるけど、登録機のUI表示に送信先番号がわかりません問題になる</p></div>
348	 </div>

Thinking

Now I have the core problem analysis (note #1) and the two countermeasures (note #3). This is very important. Let me translate and understand the key technical details:

Note #1 (2023/09/25 15:57, TRE石朋) - 問題分析 (Problem Analysis): - From registration machine (登録機) 3303 to accounting machine (会計機) 3404, sending - When the accounting machine starts restoration (復元開始), the registration machine launches a "中間取引状態チェックのループ処理" (intermediate trade state check loop process) - This time the restoration processing took a long time, so the registration machine entered a "送信失敗" (send failure) state - When pressing "再実行" (re-execute) on the registration machine, the registration machine's state became "SalesTran_WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction" - At that time, the restoration completion notification came from the accounting machine, which stopped the loop process, and executed the send completion command "Sales_NotifyCustomer" - However, since the state had changed, the command could not be executed - The screen froze in the "re-sending" screen state

Reproducibility: - Cannot reproduce on actual machine - Can reproduce in VM environment with debugging

Countermeasures (対策): Need program modification - Plan 1) Review the start/stop of the intermediate trade state check loop process - Start loop when intermediate trade restoration starts - Stop loop when entering re-send state - Stop loop when send completion notification comes - Start loop after re-send button press - Plan 2) Allow the send completion command to be executed in re-send state - Edit StateWinPOSSales.xml - ※ Can resolve the freeze issue, but causes a problem where the registration machine UI doesn't show the destination number

Note #3 (2023/09/26 17:26, TRE石朋) - Effort estimates: - Plan 1) Review of loop process start/stop: Modify 4H (UI/Command) + Test 2H - Plan 2) Allow send completion command in re-send state: Modify 2H (Setting/UI) + Test 2H

So the key technical entities: - 登録機 (registration machine/terminal) - 会計機 (accounting machine/terminal) - 中間取引 (intermediate trade / MTran) - 中間取引状態チェックのループ処理 (intermediate trade state check loop) - 復元 (restoration/restore) - State: SalesTran_WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction - Command: Sales_NotifyCustomer (送信完了コマンド = send completion command) - State machine file: StateWinPOSSales.xml - Process: POS4U.exe

Now I understand the bug. The fix zip is 2024082001_5556_SendMTranDialogFixed.zip. The naming suggests the dialog (送信ダイアログ = SendMTranDialog) was the fixed component.

So the component is likely "SendMTranDialog" - the intermediate trade (MTran) send dialog.

Now let me read the rest of the notes (the later ones - #8, and others) to understand the full timeline and what fix was decided/implemented. Let me continue reading from line 398 onwards. Note #8 was referenced. Let me find notes 4-8 and beyond.

Let me read lines 398-520 to get notes 4, 5, 6, 7, 8.

The Bug - Detailed Understanding (Note #1):

■ 問題分析 (Problem Analysis): - 登録機3303 (registration terminal 3303) → 会計機3404 (accounting terminal 3404): sending 中間取引 (intermediate trade / MTran) - When 会計機 (accounting machine) starts 復元 (restoration), 登録機 (registration machine) launches the 中間取引状態チェックのループ処理 (intermediate trade state check loop process) - This time the restoration processing took a long time, so the 登録機 (registration machine) entered 送信失敗 (send failure) state - When the user presses 再実行 (re-execute) on the registration machine, the registration machine's state became SalesTran_WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction - At that moment, the 会計機 (accounting machine) sent the restoration completion notification, which stopped the loop process, and executed the send completion command Sales_NotifyCustomer - However, since the state had changed (to the re-send state), the command could not be executed - The screen froze in the "再送信中" (re-sending) screen state

■ 再現性 (Reproducibility): - Cannot reproduce on actual hardware (実機で再現できていません) - Can reproduce in VM environment with debugging (ＶＭ環境でデバッグで再現できます)

■ 対策 (Countermeasures): Program modification required. - Plan 1) Review the start/stop of the intermediate trade state check loop process: - Start loop when intermediate trade restoration starts (中間取引復元開始から ループ処理起動) - Stop loop when entering re-send state (再送信状態になるから ループ処理停止) - Stop loop when send completion notification comes (送信完了通知が来るから ループ処理停止) - Start loop after re-send button press (再送信ボタン押下後 ループ処理起動) - Plan 2) Allow the send completion command to be executed in re-send state: - Edit StateWinPOSSales.xml - ※ Can resolve the freeze issue, but causes a problem where the registration machine UI doesn't show the destination number

Key Technical Entities to search in code:

  1. POS4U.exe - the system (Windows app)
  2. 中間取引 (MTran / MTransaction) - intermediate trade
  3. 中間取引状態チェックのループ処理 - intermediate trade state check loop
  4. State name: SalesTran_WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction
  5. Command name: Sales_NotifyCustomer (送信完了コマンド)
  6. State machine file: StateWinPOSSales.xml
  7. The dialog: SendMTranDialog (from the fix zip name)
  8. 再実行 / 再送信 (re-execute / re-send) button
  9. 復元 (restoration) - 会計機 side

Decision:

Plan 1 was chosen (note #19: "Plan 1 seems fine"). The fix was implemented as 2024082001_5556_SendMTranDialogFixed.zip.

13	{
14	    /// <summary>
15	    /// 中間取引再送信(登録機から支払い機への送信を想定)
16	    /// Sales_SendMTransactionManagementと呼べるStateを別にしたいために実装したコマンドです。
17	    /// </summary>
18	    public class Sales_ReSendMTransactionManagement : CommandWinPOSBase<StringInputData>
19	    {
20	        /// <summary>
21	        /// コンストラクタ
22	        /// </summary>
23	        public Sales_ReSendMTransactionManagement()
24	            : base(EventCodes.Sales_ReSendMTransactionManagement)
25	        {
26	        }
27	
28	        /// <summary>
29	        /// 実行時間の長い処理
30	        /// </summary>
31	        /// <param name="posData">POSData</param>
32	        /// <returns>True:長い/False:短い</returns>
33	        protected override bool OnIsTimeConsumingTask(POSData posData)
34	        {
35	            return true;
36	        }
37	
38	        /// <summary>
39	        /// 実行
40	        /// </summary>
41	        /// <param name="deviceId">デバイスId</param>
42	        /// <param name="inputData">入力値</param>
43	        /// <param name="posData">POSData</param>
44	        /// <returns>true:正常/false:異常</returns>
45	        protected override bool OnExecute(string deviceId, StringInputData inputData, POSData posData)
46	        {
47	            var iMTranServiceWinPOS = (IMTranServiceWinPOS)Factory.CreatePlugin(FrameworkPluginIds.DeviceManager).GetDevice(DeviceIds.MTranService);
48	
49	            if (iMTranServiceWinPOS == null)
50	            {
51	                return false;
52	            }
53	
54	            string mTransactionId = string.Empty;
55	
56	            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(inputData.InputData))
57	            {
58	                // 中間取引を保存して中間取引Idを取得
59	                MTranObject mTranObj = new MTranObject();
60	                mTransactionId = mTranObj.Save(posData, PauseTypes.SemiSelf);
61	            }
62	            else
63	            {
64	                if (!MTranObject.IsValidMTransactionId(inputData.InputData))
65	                {
66	                    posData.UserData.SetError(MessageIds.ErrorInputIllegal);
67	                    return false;
68	                }
69	
70	                // InputDataから中間取引Idを取得
71	                mTransactionId = inputData.InputData;
72	            }
73	
74	            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(mTransactionId))
75	            {
76	                return false;
77	            }
78	
79	            var tran = (SalesTran)posData.CurrentTran;
80	
81	            // 中間取引Idをセット
82	            iMTranServiceWinPOS.TargetMTransactionId = mTransactionId;
83	
84	            // 中間取引受付可能問い合わせを行う。
85	            if (!iMTranServiceWinPOS.ReInquiryGetMTransactionResult(posData.UserData.DataAccessParameter))
86	            {
87	                tran.ChangeState(SalesTranStates.FailedRequestGetMTransaction);
88	                return true;
89	            }
90	
91	            tran.ChangeState(SalesTranStates.WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction);
92	
93	            return true;
94	        }
95	    }
96	}

Assistant2026-07-03T01:10:28.024Z
Thinking

This is the smoking gun for Plan 2 / the state-machine guard. Let me analyze:

State machine (StateWinPOSSales.xml):

  • Line 210-216: SalesTran_SendMTransaction state allows: Sales_NotifyCustomer, Sales_ReSendMTransactionManagement, Sales_LoadMTransactionManagement, Sales_Total, Sales_ChangeDisplay. So in SendMTransaction state, the completion command IS allowed.

  • Line 217-223: SalesTran_NotFoundInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction, SalesTran_FailedRequestGetMTransaction (the "no accounting machine found" / "send failure" states) allow: Sales_NotifyCustomer, Sales_ReSendMTransactionManagement, etc. So in the failure state, the operator CAN press re-send (再実行) OR notify.

  • Line 224-226: SalesTran_WaitingInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction, SalesTran_WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction, SalesTran_WaitingRequestGetMTransaction → only allows Sales_Total.

THIS IS THE KEY: In the WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction state (re-send waiting), the ONLY command permitted is Sales_Total. Sales_NotifyCustomer is NOT permitted in this state!

So this confirms the bug report's step 6 exactly: when the loop fires Sales_NotifyCustomer while the state is WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction, the state machine REJECTS it (it's not in the allowed command list for that state). The command can't execute → state stays WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction → dialog stuck on "re-sending" → frozen.

Note that Sales_NotifyCustomer IS allowed in: - SendMTransaction (line 212) - NotFoundInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction and FailedRequestGetMTransaction (line 219)

But NOT in WaitingInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction or WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction (line 224 — only Sales_Total).

Wait — this means Sales_NotifyCustomer is not allowed in EITHER waiting state (initial or re-send). So how does the normal flow work? Let me think.

Normal flow (no bug): 1. Initial send: state → WaitingInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction (waiting for can-accept result) 2. The can-accept result arrives (asynchronously). This must transition the state back to SendMTransaction (or somewhere) where Sales_NotifyCustomer IS allowed. 3. Then Sales_NotifyCustomer fires → Fixed.

So the normal flow is: the loop/notification transitions state from WaitingInquiry... → SendMTransaction (or the result callback does ChangeState), THEN fires Sales_NotifyCustomer.

The bug scenario: 1. Send fails → state FailedRequestGetMTransaction (line 217 — Sales_NotifyCustomer allowed here) 2. Operator presses 再実行 → Sales_ReSendMTransactionManagement → state WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction (line 224 — only Sales_Total) 3. Meanwhile the restoration-complete notification arrives → the callback tries to fire Sales_NotifyCustomer (or transition then notify).

The bug: the notification/loop fires Sales_NotifyCustomer while still in WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction (the re-inquiry hasn't returned yet, OR the state didn't transition back to a state that allows NotifyCustomer). Since WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction only allows Sales_Total, Sales_NotifyCustomer is rejected → frozen.

This is the race condition. Plan 2 would fix it by adding Sales_NotifyCustomer to line 224's state. But the chosen Plan 1 is to fix the loop timing: stop the loop when entering re-send state, restart after re-send button pressed — so the loop doesn't fire NotifyCustomer at the wrong moment.

Now, the question the workflow is investigating: does the current repo code reflect Plan 1 (fixed) or is it still buggy?

The state machine XML (line 224) currently does NOT include Sales_NotifyCustomer in WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction. If Plan 1 was implemented (and Plan 2 rejected), the XML would stay as-is (no NotifyCustomer in re-send state), and the FIX would be in the loop/dialog code. So the XML being "un-fixed" (no NotifyCustomer in re-send state) is actually CONSISTENT with Plan 1 being the chosen approach.

So the fix must be in: - The loop start/stop (so it stops when entering re-send state, restarts after re-send button) - OR the dialog (SendMTransactionDialog — the fix zip is named after it)

The fix zip is named ..._SendMTranDialogFixed.zip, strongly suggesting the fix is in the DIALOG code (SendMTransactionDialog.xaml.cs). This aligns with Plan 1's "stop loop when entering re-send state, restart after re-send button" being implemented in the dialog (which handles the re-send button and shows the loop/timer).

114	        /// <summary>
115	        /// 画面更新
116	        /// </summary>
117	        /// <param name="posData">POSデータ</param>
118	        public override void UpdateDisplay(POSData posData)
119	        {
120	            this.IsEnabled = true;
121	
122	            lock (_lockObj)
123	            {
124	                if (this._isFinished)
125	                {
126	                    return;
127	                }
128	            }
129	
130	            var state = posData.CurrentTran.CurrentState;
131	
132	            // 中間取引受付可能な会計機なし
133	            if (state == SalesTranStates.NotFoundInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction)
134	            {
135	                if (this.CheckMTransactionOperationState(this.MTranServiceWinPOS.TargetMTransactionId) != CheckOperationStateDiv.Notyet)
136	                {
137	                    // 中間取引が取得済みまたは存在しない。
138	                    return;
139	                }
140	
141	                this.txbMessage.Text = "会計機は全て利用中です。"
142	                    + Environment.NewLine
143	                    + "再実行してください。";
144	                this.SetButtonsEnabled(false, true, true);
145	                this.StopTimer();
146	
147	                UIPOSLibrary.PlaySelfErrorBeepSound();
148	            }
149	
150	            // 中間取引取得リクエストの送信に失敗
151	            else if (state == SalesTranStates.FailedRequestGetMTransaction)
152	            {
153	                if (this.CheckMTransactionOperationState(this.MTranServiceWinPOS.TargetMTransactionId) != CheckOperationStateDiv.Notyet)
154	                {
155	                    // 中間取引が取得済みまたは存在しない。
156	                    return;
157	                }
158	
159	                this.txbMessage.Text = "会計対象取引Idの送信時にエラーが発生しました。";
160	                this.SetButtonsEnabled(false, true, true);
161	                this.StopTimer();
162	            }
163	
164	            // 中間取引送信
165	            else if (state == SalesTranStates.SendMTransaction)
166	            {
167	                if (!this._timer.Enabled)
168	                {
169	                    if (this.txbMessage.Text != this._messageTimeout)
170	                    {
171	                        this._posData = posData;
172	                        this.ReStartTimer();
173	                    }
174	                }
175	                else
176	                {
177	                    this.SetButtonsEnabled(false, false, false);
178	                }
179	
180	                if (this._isChecking)
181	                {
182	                    return;
183	                }
184	
185	                // 中間取引送信処理
186	                ThreadPool.QueueUserWorkItem((object callBack) =>
187	                {
188	                    string targetMTransactionId = this.MTranServiceWinPOS?.TargetMTransactionId;
189	
190	                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(targetMTransactionId))
191	                    {
192	                        // 該当の中間取引が取得されるまで待機
193	                        while (true)
194	                        {
195	                            this._isChecking = true;
196	                            if (this.CheckMTransactionOperationState(targetMTransactionId) != CheckOperationStateDiv.Notyet)
197	                            {
198	                                this._isChecking = false;
199	                                break;
200	                            }
201	
202	                            this.Dispatcher.Invoke((MethodInvoker)(() =>
203	                            {
204	                                if (this._timer.Enabled)
205	                                {
206	                                    this.txbMessage.Text = "会計対象取引送信待機中です。";
207	                                }
208	                            }));
209	
210	                            Thread.Sleep(500);
211	                        }
212	                    }
213	                });
214	            }
215	        }
216

310	        private void OnElapsed_TimersTimer(object sender, ElapsedEventArgs e)
311	        {
312	            this.Dispatcher.Invoke((MethodInvoker)(() =>
313	            {
314	                this.txbMessage.Text = this._messageTimeout;
315	
316	                // タイマーの停止・解放
317	                this.StopTimer();
318	
319	                ResultValue<bool> inquiryResult = null;
320	
321	                Task.Factory.StartNew(() =>
322	                {
323	                    inquiryResult = this.MTranServiceWinPOS.ReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction(_posData.UserData.DataAccessParameter);
324	                }).Wait(2000);
325	
326	                if ((inquiryResult == null || !inquiryResult.IsSuccess) &&
327	                    this.MTranServiceWinPOS.BeforeCanAcceptTerminal != null)
328	                {
329	                    this.txbMessage.Text = this.MTranServiceWinPOS.BeforeCanAcceptTerminal.Description + "番通信中にエラーが発生しました、確認ください。";
330	                    this.SetButtonsEnabled(false, true, true);
331	                }
332	                else if (inquiryResult.Value)
333	                {
334	                    var parameter = new DataAccessParameter(
335	                                            _posData.UserData.DataAccessParameter.CompanyCode,
336	                                            _posData.UserData.DataAccessParameter.StoreCode,
337	                                            this.MTranServiceWinPOS.BeforeCanAcceptTerminal.PaymentStationTerminalNo);
338	
339	                    var result = this.MTranServiceWinPOS.UpdateMTransactionManagementOperationState(
340	                                                parameter,
341	                                                this.MTranServiceWinPOS.TargetMTransactionId,
342	                                                2,
343	                                                _posData.UserData.IsTrainingMode,
344	                                                this.MTranServiceWinPOS.BeforeCanAcceptTerminal.PaymentStationTerminalNo,
345	                                                true);
346	                }
347	                else
348	                {
349	                    // 再実行ボタンと自分で復元ボタンを押せるようにする
350	                    this.SetButtonsEnabled(false, true, true);
351	                }
352	            }));
353	        }

376	        /// <param name="btnCloseEnabled">閉じるボタン有効</param>
377	        /// <param name="btnRetryEnabled">再実行ボタン有効</param>
378	        /// <param name="btnRestoreEnabled">自分で復元ボタン有効</param>
379	        private void SetButtonsEnabled(bool btnCloseEnabled, bool btnRetryEnabled, bool btnRestoreEnabled)
380	        {
381	            this.btnClose.IsEnabled = btnCloseEnabled;
382	            this.btnRetry.IsEnabled = btnRetryEnabled;
383	            this.btnRestore.IsEnabled = btnRestoreEnabled;
384	        }
385	
386	        /// <summary>
387	        /// 中間取引が取得されたかチェック
388	        /// </summary>
389	        /// <param name="targetMTransactionId">対象中間取引Id</param>
390	        /// <returns>中間取引取得チェック区分</returns>
391	        private CheckOperationStateDiv CheckMTransactionOperationState(string targetMTransactionId)
392	        {
393	            var accessParameter = new DataAccessParameter(WinPOSFrameworkSettingValues.CompanyCode.Value, WinPOSFrameworkSettingValues.StoreCode.Value, WinPOSFrameworkSettingValues.TerminalNo.Value);
394	
395	            var result = MTransactionManagementAccessor.GetMTransactionOperationStateRow(accessParameter, targetMTransactionId);
396	            if (result == null)
397	            {
398	                lock (_lockObj)
399	                {
400	                    this._isFinished = true;
401	                }
402	
403	                this.Dispatcher.Invoke((MethodInvoker)(() =>
404	                {
405	                    Logger.Error("MTransaction Not Found." + " MTransactionId:" + targetMTransactionId);
406	                    this.txbMessage.Text = "会計対象取引が存在しません。" + " MTransactionId:" + targetMTransactionId;
407	                    this.SetButtonsEnabled(true, false, false);
408	                    this.StopTimer();
409	                }));
410	
411	                // 存在しないので処理を中断させる。
412	                return CheckOperationStateDiv.NotFound;
413	            }
414	            else
415	            {
416	                // 自分復元
417	                if (result.OperationState == 1 &&
418	                    result.LastUpdateTerminalNo == WinPOSFrameworkSettingValues.TerminalNo.Value)
419	                {
420	                    lock (_lockObj)
421	                    {
422	                        this._isFinished = true;
423	                    }
424	
425	                    this.StopTimer();
426	                    return CheckOperationStateDiv.Already;
427	                }
428	
429	                // 該当の中間取引が取得された。
430	                if (result.OperationState == 2)
431	                {
432	                    lock (_lockObj)
433	                    {
434	                        this._isFinished = true;
435	                    }
436	
437	                    this.Dispatcher.Invoke((MethodInvoker)(() =>
438	                    {
439	                        string message = string.Empty;
440	
441	                        if (result.LastUpdateTerminalNo == WinPOSFrameworkSettingValues.TerminalNo.Value)
442	                        {
443	                            message = "自端末で取引を復元しました。";
444	                            this.txbMessage.Text = message;
445	                            this.SetButtonsEnabled(true, false, false);
446	                        }
447	                        else
448	                        {
449	                            UIPOSLibrary.AcceptEvent(DeviceIds.TouchDisplay.Id, EventCodes.Sales_NotifyCustomer.Code, null);
450	                        }
451	                    }));
452	
453	                    this.StopTimer();
454	                    return CheckOperationStateDiv.Already;
455	                }
456	            }
457	
458	            return CheckOperationStateDiv.Notyet;
459	        }
460	    }
461	}

Now I understand the dialog and the loop mechanism completely. Let me analyze:

SendMTransactionDialog.xaml.cs — the dialog and the loop:

The dialog has a polling loop (the "中間取引状態チェックのループ処理"): - Line 33: private readonly System.Timers.Timer _timer — a timeout timer - Lines 64-68: timer set to SendMTransactionTimeoutMillisecond, AutoReset = false (one-shot timeout) - Lines 186-213: the actual polling loop. In UpdateDisplay when state == SendMTransaction, it queues a ThreadPool work item that runs a while(true) loop (line 193) polling CheckMTransactionOperationState(targetMTransactionId) every 500ms (Thread.Sleep(500), line 210). It breaks when the MTransaction is "obtained" (operation state != Notyet).

The completion path (line 449): When CheckMTransactionOperationState finds OperationState == 2 (該当の中間取引が取得された = the MTransaction was obtained/accepted by an accounting machine) AND it's NOT the local terminal, it fires:

UIPOSLibrary.AcceptEvent(DeviceIds.TouchDisplay.Id, EventCodes.Sales_NotifyCustomer.Code, null);

This is the Sales_NotifyCustomer command — the 送信完了コマンド!

So the loop, on detecting the MTransaction was obtained, fires Sales_NotifyCustomer. This is the loop that fires the notify command.

  1. So the flow is:

  5. Sales_NotifyCustomer (allowed in SendMTransaction state, line 212 of XML) → state Fixed. Dialog closes.

So the NORMAL flow: state goes to SendMTransaction, loop runs, detects acceptance, fires NotifyCustomer (allowed), → Fixed.

Actually, re-reading the bug report note #1: "会計機側復元開始から、登録機は中間取引状態チェックのループ処理を起動します" — "From the time the accounting machine starts restoration, the registration machine launches the MTransaction state-check loop process."

Hmm, but actually maybe the scenario is different. Let me re-read once more very carefully:

  1. 会計機 starts 復元 (restoration)
  2. 登録機 launches the MTransaction state-check loop (because restoration started)
  3. Restoration took long → 登録機 went to 送信失敗 (send failure) state.

The loop... hmm, but the loop runs in SendMTransaction state via UpdateDisplay. When state changes to FailedRequestGetMTransaction, UpdateDisplay would take the failure branch (line 151), but the while(true) loop on the ThreadPool might STILL be running (it's on a background thread, line 186 ThreadPool.QueueUserWorkItem)!

Look at the loop (lines 186-213): it's queued on the ThreadPool and runs while(true). It only breaks when CheckMTransactionOperationState(targetMTransactionId) != Notyet (line 196). The _isChecking flag (line 195) guards re-entry (line 180).

So the background loop keeps polling every 500ms until the MTransaction is obtained. When it's obtained (OperationState==2), CheckMTransactionOperationState itself (line 449) fires Sales_NotifyCustomer via AcceptEvent.

Wait — line 449 is INSIDE CheckMTransactionOperationState, which is called from the loop (line 196). So when the loop detects the MTransaction is obtained, it fires Sales_NotifyCustomer.

So here's the race: - The background loop (started in SendMTransaction state) keeps running even after state changed to FailedRequestGetMTransaction and then to WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction (after re-execute). The loop doesn't check the SalesTran state — it only checks the MTransaction operation state in the DB (via CheckMTransactionOperationState). - When the accounting machine finishes restoration and accepts the MTransaction, CheckMTransactionOperationState returns OperationState==2 → fires Sales_NotifyCustomer (line 449). - BUT at that moment, the SalesTran state is WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction (because the operator just pressed re-execute). - Sales_NotifyCustomer is NOT allowed in WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction (XML line 224) → REJECTED → state stays → dialog frozen on "再実行中です。".

THIS IS THE BUG. The background polling loop (lines 186-213) fires Sales_NotifyCustomer regardless of the current SalesTran state. If the SalesTran state is a waiting state where NotifyCustomer isn't permitted, the command is rejected and the dialog freezes.

This perfectly matches the bug report: - "会計機から復元完了の通知が来て" = the loop detected the MTransaction was obtained (restoration complete) - "上記ループ処理を停止し" = the loop's while(true) breaks (CheckMTransactionOperationState returned != Notyet, line 196-199) - "送信完了コマンド「Sales_NotifyCustomer」を実行し" = line 449 fires AcceptEvent(Sales_NotifyCustomer) - "しかし、状態が変わりましたので、コマンド実行できなかった" = state is WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction, NotifyCustomer not permitted → rejected - "画面は再送信中の画面のままで固まった" = dialog stuck on "再実行中です。" message

Key technical conclusion (CONFIRMED by adversarial verification): - The freeze is a state/loop decoupling in SendMTransactionDialog.xaml.cs. - The "中間取引状態チェックのループ処理" is a ThreadPool.QueueUserWorkItem with while(true) loop (lines 186-213), spawned when state==SendMTransaction. - The loop polls OperationState every 500ms, and when OperationState==2 fires Sales_NotifyCustomer at line 449 UNCONDITIONALLY (no state guard). - BtnRetry_Click (再実行, lines 252-265) fires ReSendMTransactionManagement → state WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction (line 91 of that command), but only stops the TIMER, NOT the polling loop. - StateWinPOSSales.xml:224-226 — in WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction, only Sales_Total is permitted; Sales_NotifyCustomer is NOT. - So when the stale loop fires Sales_NotifyCustomer into WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction, the state machine rejects it → Sales_NotifyCustomer.cs:31 (ChangeState Fixed) never runs → dialog stuck on "再送信中" → frozen.

Current code state: BUGGY (pre-fix). Neither Plan 1 (loop stop on re-send) nor Plan 2 (add NotifyCustomer to waiting state in XML) is present. This matches the timeline (note #28, 2025/09: still asking whether to recreate on new base; status still 新規/New; the fix zip was delivered separately for acceptance testing but never merged).

Additional richness from the workflow: - SalesTran.cs:1473 (CompleteReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction) and SalesTran.cs:1536 (CompleteRequestGetMTransaction) are the event-driven transitions INTO SendMTransaction — this is the "restart loop after re-send" path of Plan 1. - Device_StatusUpdate.cs:119-133 routes async device results by current state (WaitingInquiry→CompleteInquiry, WaitingReInquiry→CompleteReInquiry, WaitingRequestGet→CompleteRequestGet) — this is the event-driven path that RACES with the dialog's DB-polling path. - MTranService: restoration-complete is NOT a push; it's decoded from the poll response (MTranServiceListener.cs:394-399 encoder ↔ MTranServiceWinPOS.cs:1672-1690/779-797 decoder). The device layer is clean — no defect there. - The race window: ReInquiryGetMTransactionResult is async (MTranServiceWinPOS.cs:828 returns true immediately), so there's a window where state is WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction but the old polling loop is still alive. - _isFinished is latched true at line 434 (before line 449 dispatch), short-circuiting UpdateDisplay at lines 124-128, and the line-449 else branch enables no buttons → dialog permanently open.

Assistant2026-07-03T01:33:33.616Z

#5556 分析报告：中間取引送信時画面表示が固まる

我已阅读该 Redmine 票据的 HTML 全文、按时间线理清了问题，并对代码做了只读分析（未编译、未执行）。结论经过一个对抗式验证工作流的独立复核（verdict = CONFIRMED）。下面分四部分说明。

一、问题与时间线（票据内容）

票据基本信息：不具合，系统 POS4U.exe，分类「不具合」，前端「ペイメントグループ」，状态至今仍是 新規，已过 1016 天。

冻结场景（note #1，TRE石朋，2023/09/25）—— 注册机(3303)向会計機(3404)发送中間取引(MTransaction)：

  1. 会計機开始 復元（恢复）。
  2. 登录侧随即启动 「中間取引状態チェックのループ処理」（轮询 MTransaction 状态的循环）。
  3. 因復元耗时长，登录侧进入 送信失敗 状态。
  4. 操作员按下 再実行 → 状态变为 SalesTran_WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction。
  5. 此时 会計機发来復元完成通知 → 循环停止 → 执行送信完成命令 Sales_NotifyCustomer。
  6. 但状态已变（进入再送信等待态），命令 无法执行（被状态机拒绝）。
  7. 画面卡在「再送信中」对话框 → 冻结。

复现性：实机无法复现，VM + 调试可复现（典型 竞态/时序 问题）。

两个对策方案（note #3）： - 案1（采用，note #19 2024/08/07）：重新审视循环的 起停时机（復元开始→启动；进入再送信态→停止；送信完成通知→停止；再送信按钮按下后→重启）。 - 案2（否决）：改 StateWinPOSSales.xml，让再送信态也能执行 Sales_NotifyCustomer。缺点：登录侧 UI 不再显示送信先番号。

交付物：附件 2024082001_5556_SendMTranDialogFixed.zip（base 2024071001，修复对象是 Dialog）。note #22 起进入受入测试（优先度低）；note #28（2025/09）仍在问「是否需要在新基线模块上重新制作」——说明该修复从未合入主干。

二、代码根因（已定位）

冻结的本质是 「状态机」与「轮询循环」解耦，缺陷全部集中在 SendMTransactionDialog.xaml.cs。

1. 那个「循环」就在 Dialog 里

SendMTransactionDialog.xaml.cs:186-213：仅在 state == SendMTransaction 时，用 ThreadPool.QueueUserWorkItem 起一个 while(true) 线程，每 500ms（:210）轮询数据库里的 MTransaction OperationState。

2. 循环体一旦发现「已被会計機取得」就无条件发送完成命令

SendMTransactionDialog.xaml.cs:449（在 CheckMTransactionOperationState 内，OperationState==2 且非本端分支）：

UIPOSLibrary.AcceptEvent(DeviceIds.TouchDisplay.Id, EventCodes.Sales_NotifyCustomer.Code, null);

这里没有任何对当前 SalesTran 状态的判断 —— 这就是误触发点。

3. 「再実行」按钮只停了定时器，没停轮询循环

SendMTransactionDialog.xaml.cs:252-265（BtnRetry_Click）：触发 Sales_ReSendMTransactionManagement 后只调 this.StopTimer()（:264）。对比 BtnClose_Click:240 和 BtnRestore_Click:280 至少还置了 _isChecking=false，再実行按钮连这一步都没做，且没有任何取消令牌能中断那个后台 while(true)。

4. 再送信命令把状态切到「等待态」

Sales_ReSendMTransactionManagement.cs:91 → ChangeState(WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction)。而初始发送走的是另一个状态 Sales_SendMTransactionManagement.cs:107 → WaitingInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction。

5. 状态机在该等待态里不允许 Sales_NotifyCustomer

StateWinPOSSales.xml:224-226：

<State Ids="SalesTran_WaitingInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction,SalesTran_WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction,SalesTran_WaitingRequestGetMTransaction">
  <Command Id="Sales_Total" />
</State>

该组 只允许 Sales_Total。而 Sales_NotifyCustomer 在 SendMTransaction（:212）和 失敗/未発見 态（:219）是允许的——唯独在等待/再送信态不允许。Sales_NotifyCustomer.OnExecute 仅做 ChangeState(Fixed)（Sales_NotifyCustomer.cs:31），它一旦被拒，交易就再无合法出口进入 Fixed。

串起来 = 冻结

后台循环仍在跑 → 看到 OperationState==2 → :449 发 Sales_NotifyCustomer → 但此刻状态已被「再実行」切到 WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction → 状态机拒绝 → Fixed 永远到不了 → 且 :434 已把 _isFinished 置 true（使 :124-128 的 UpdateDisplay 短路），:449 分支又不启用任何按钮 → 「再送信中」对话框永久卡死。与票据 7 步描述逐一吻合。

竞态窗口：再送信命令调用的 ReInquiryGetMTransactionResult 是异步的（MTranServiceWinPOS.cs:828 立即 return true），所以存在「状态已是 WaitingReInquiry、但旧循环还活着」的窗口——这正是实机难复现、VM 调试可复现的原因。

三、关键澄清（验证工作流的额外发现）

  • 设备层（MTranService）是干净的，没有缺陷。復元完成不是推送，而是轮询响应里解码出来的（编码 MTranServiceListener.cs:394-399 ↔ 解码 MTranServiceWinPOS.cs:1672-1690 / :779-797）。它只抛一个通用的 Device_StatusUpdate 事件，既不直接调 Sales_NotifyCustomer，也不停循环。缺陷全在 Dialog 的循环生命周期里。
  • 事件驱动路径与 Dialog 的 DB 轮询路径 并存且相互竞争：Device_StatusUpdate.cs:119-133 按当前态路由（WaitingInquiry→CompleteInquiry、WaitingReInquiry→CompleteReInquiry、WaitingRequestGet→CompleteRequestGet）；而 SalesTran.cs:1473（CompleteReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction，restoreStatus==2）与 SalesTran.cs:1536（CompleteRequestGetMTransaction）才是把状态 切回 SendMTransaction 的地方——也就是案1「再送信后重启循环」要落到的位置。
  • 三个命令文件（Sales_SendMTransactionManagement / Sales_ReSendMTransactionManagement / Sales_NotifyCustomer）单独看都是正确的，只是薄薄的状态切换驱动器。

四、当前代码是「已修复」还是「仍有缺陷」？

结论：当前仓库代码仍是「修复前（buggy）」版本，案1 和案2 均未落地。 直接证据（验证器逐条开文件核对过）：

案1 的要求 当前代码 是否落地
进入再送信态 → 停止循环 BtnRetry_Click:252-265 只停定时器，无循环停止/取消 ❌
循环别在错误状态发命令 :449 无条件发 Sales_NotifyCustomer ❌
再送信后重启循环 需状态回到 SendMTransaction，逻辑本身在，但没有配合循环停止的重启编排 ⚠️ 半成品
案2 的要求 当前代码 是否落地
在 StateWinPOSSales.xml:224-226 的等待态组里加 Sales_NotifyCustomer 仍只有 Sales_Total ❌（符合「案2被否决」）

这与时间线完全吻合：修复只作为 2024082001_5556_SendMTranDialogFixed.zip 单独交付做受入测试，从未合入这份源码树；note #28（2025/09）仍在问要不要在新基线上重做，票据状态至今仍是「新規」。

备注：这是非 git 的只读快照，无法从构建号 100% 证明它是 base 2024071001；但 Dialog 只有一个 .xaml.cs（无 partial class 隐藏逻辑），循环生命周期已完整覆盖，且呈现的正是 buggy 模式——所以「未修复」的判断是可靠的。

一句话总结

画面冻结是因为 SendMTransactionDialog 里的后台 while(true) 轮询循环在「再実行」后没有被停止，于「再送信等待态」错误地触发了 Sales_NotifyCustomer，而该状态在 StateWinPOSSales.xml 里不允许此命令，导致交易永远到不了 Fixed、对话框卡死。根因与对策「案1」完全对应，但当前代码尚未应用该修复。

Brainstorming Ideas Into Designs

Help turn ideas into fully formed designs and specs through natural collaborative dialogue.

Start by understanding the current project context, then ask questions one at a time to refine the idea. Once you understand what you're building, present the design and get user approval.

Do NOT invoke any implementation skill, write any code, scaffold any project, or take any implementation action until you have presented a design and the user has approved it. This applies to EVERY project regardless of perceived simplicity.

Anti-Pattern: "This Is Too Simple To Need A Design"

Every project goes through this process. A todo list, a single-function utility, a config change — all of them. "Simple" projects are where unexamined assumptions cause the most wasted work. The design can be short (a few sentences for truly simple projects), but you MUST present it and get approval.

Checklist

You MUST create a task for each of these items and complete them in order:

  1. Explore project context — check files, docs, recent commits
  2. Offer the visual companion just-in-time — NOT upfront. The first time a question would genuinely be clearer shown than described, offer it then (its own message); on approval its browser tab opens for you. If no visual question ever arises, never offer it. See the Visual Companion section below.
  3. Ask clarifying questions — one at a time, understand purpose/constraints/success criteria
  4. Propose 2-3 approaches — with trade-offs and your recommendation
  5. Present design — in sections scaled to their complexity, get user approval after each section
  6. Write design doc — save to docs/superpowers/specs/YYYY-MM-DD-<topic>-design.md and commit
  7. Spec self-review — quick inline check for placeholders, contradictions, ambiguity, scope (see below)
  8. User reviews written spec — ask user to review the spec file before proceeding
  9. Transition to implementation — invoke writing-plans skill to create implementation plan

Process Flow

digraph brainstorming {
    "Explore project context" [shape=box];
    "Ask clarifying questions" [shape=box];
    "Propose 2-3 approaches" [shape=box];
    "Present design sections" [shape=box];
    "User approves design?" [shape=diamond];
    "Write design doc" [shape=box];
    "Spec self-review\n(fix inline)" [shape=box];
    "User reviews spec?" [shape=diamond];
    "Invoke writing-plans skill" [shape=doublecircle];

    "Explore project context" -> "Ask clarifying questions";
    "Ask clarifying questions" -> "Propose 2-3 approaches";
    "Propose 2-3 approaches" -> "Present design sections";
    "Present design sections" -> "User approves design?";
    "User approves design?" -> "Present design sections" [label="no, revise"];
    "User approves design?" -> "Write design doc" [label="yes"];
    "Write design doc" -> "Spec self-review\n(fix inline)";
    "Spec self-review\n(fix inline)" -> "User reviews spec?";
    "User reviews spec?" -> "Write design doc" [label="changes requested"];
    "User reviews spec?" -> "Invoke writing-plans skill" [label="approved"];
}

The terminal state is invoking writing-plans. Do NOT invoke frontend-design, mcp-builder, or any other implementation skill. The ONLY skill you invoke after brainstorming is writing-plans.

The Process

Understanding the idea:

  • Check out the current project state first (files, docs, recent commits)
  • Before asking detailed questions, assess scope: if the request describes multiple independent subsystems (e.g., "build a platform with chat, file storage, billing, and analytics"), flag this immediately. Don't spend questions refining details of a project that needs to be decomposed first.
  • If the project is too large for a single spec, help the user decompose into sub-projects: what are the independent pieces, how do they relate, what order should they be built? Then brainstorm the first sub-project through the normal design flow. Each sub-project gets its own spec → plan → implementation cycle.
  • For appropriately-scoped projects, ask questions one at a time to refine the idea
  • Prefer multiple choice questions when possible, but open-ended is fine too
  • Only one question per message - if a topic needs more exploration, break it into multiple questions
  • Focus on understanding: purpose, constraints, success criteria

Exploring approaches:

  • Propose 2-3 different approaches with trade-offs
  • Present options conversationally with your recommendation and reasoning
  • Lead with your recommended option and explain why

Presenting the design:

  • Once you believe you understand what you're building, present the design
  • Scale each section to its complexity: a few sentences if straightforward, up to 200-300 words if nuanced
  • Ask after each section whether it looks right so far
  • Cover: architecture, components, data flow, error handling, testing
  • Be ready to go back and clarify if something doesn't make sense

Design for isolation and clarity:

  • Break the system into smaller units that each have one clear purpose, communicate through well-defined interfaces, and can be understood and tested independently
  • For each unit, you should be able to answer: what does it do, how do you use it, and what does it depend on?
  • Can someone understand what a unit does without reading its internals? Can you change the internals without breaking consumers? If not, the boundaries need work.
  • Smaller, well-bounded units are also easier for you to work with - you reason better about code you can hold in context at once, and your edits are more reliable when files are focused. When a file grows large, that's often a signal that it's doing too much.

Working in existing codebases:

  • Explore the current structure before proposing changes. Follow existing patterns.
  • Where existing code has problems that affect the work (e.g., a file that's grown too large, unclear boundaries, tangled responsibilities), include targeted improvements as part of the design - the way a good developer improves code they're working in.
  • Don't propose unrelated refactoring. Stay focused on what serves the current goal.

After the Design

Documentation:

  • Write the validated design (spec) to docs/superpowers/specs/YYYY-MM-DD-<topic>-design.md
  • (User preferences for spec location override this default)
  • Use elements-of-style:writing-clearly-and-concisely skill if available
  • Commit the design document to git

Spec Self-Review: After writing the spec document, look at it with fresh eyes:

  1. Placeholder scan: Any "TBD", "TODO", incomplete sections, or vague requirements? Fix them.
  2. Internal consistency: Do any sections contradict each other? Does the architecture match the feature descriptions?
  3. Scope check: Is this focused enough for a single implementation plan, or does it need decomposition?
  4. Ambiguity check: Could any requirement be interpreted two different ways? If so, pick one and make it explicit.

Fix any issues inline. No need to re-review — just fix and move on.

User Review Gate: After the spec review loop passes, ask the user to review the written spec before proceeding:

"Spec written and committed to <path>. Please review it and let me know if you want to make any changes before we start writing out the implementation plan."

Wait for the user's response. If they request changes, make them and re-run the spec review loop. Only proceed once the user approves.

Implementation:

  • Invoke the writing-plans skill to create a detailed implementation plan
  • Do NOT invoke any other skill. writing-plans is the next step.

Key Principles

  • One question at a time - Don't overwhelm with multiple questions
  • Multiple choice preferred - Easier to answer than open-ended when possible
  • YAGNI ruthlessly - Remove unnecessary features from all designs
  • Explore alternatives - Always propose 2-3 approaches before settling
  • Incremental validation - Present design, get approval before moving on
  • Be flexible - Go back and clarify when something doesn't make sense

Visual Companion

A browser-based companion for showing mockups, diagrams, and visual options during brainstorming. Available as a tool — not a mode. Accepting the companion means it's available for questions that benefit from visual treatment; it does NOT mean every question goes through the browser.

Offering the companion (just-in-time): Do NOT offer it upfront. Wait until a question would genuinely be clearer shown than told — a real mockup / layout / diagram question, not merely a UI topic. The first time that happens, offer it then, as its own message:

"This next part might be easier if I show you — I can put together mockups, diagrams, and comparisons in a browser tab as we go. It's still new and can be token-intensive. Want me to? I'll open it for you."

This offer MUST be its own message. Only the offer — no clarifying question, summary, or other content. Wait for the user's response. If they accept, start the server with --open so their browser opens to the first screen automatically. If they decline, continue text-only and don't offer again unless they raise it.

Per-question decision: Even after the user accepts, decide FOR EACH QUESTION whether to use the browser or the terminal. The test: would the user understand this better by seeing it than reading it?

  • Use the browser for content that IS visual — mockups, wireframes, layout comparisons, architecture diagrams, side-by-side visual designs
  • Use the terminal for content that is text — requirements questions, conceptual choices, tradeoff lists, A/B/C/D text options, scope decisions

A question about a UI topic is not automatically a visual question. "What does personality mean in this context?" is a conceptual question — use the terminal. "Which wizard layout works better?" is a visual question — use the browser.

If they agree to the companion, read the detailed guide before proceeding: skills/brainstorming/visual-companion.md

先说结论：我倾向于「值得重新考虑」，但建议不是在「案1 vs 案2」里二选一，而是把方向从「靠时序停循环」改为「让命令对状态自洽」。 不过要给出确切建议，我需要先确认你说的 Lanself 具体指什么——这个词在当前代码库里搜不到（节点类型只有 GoSemiSelfRegister(07) / GoSemiSelfPaymentStation(08) 这类「GOセミセルフ 登録機/会計機」）。我先按「LAN 组网下的自助(セルフ/セミセルフ) 登録機↔会計機 MTransaction 场景」来理解，如果不对请纠正。

为什么我倾向重新考虑（基于代码，与 Lanself 无关的部分）：

  • 案1 的本质是「用时机去规避竞态」——它要求「进入再送信态前/后正好把后台 while(true) 循环停掉再重启」。但这个循环（SendMTransactionDialog.xaml.cs:186-213）是裸 ThreadPool + Thread.Sleep(500) 轮询，没有取消令牌，BtnRetry_Click 也只停了定时器（:264）。要让「停/重启」在所有时序下都成立，改动面不小且本身仍是竞态敏感的。
  • 真正的病根是「循环不看状态就发命令」：:449 无条件 AcceptEvent(Sales_NotifyCustomer)。一个更稳、改动更小的方向（姑且叫案3）是：在发命令前判断当前 SalesTran 状态是否允许（或干脆让 CheckMTransactionOperationState 在非 SendMTransaction 态时只更新 UI/重启等待，不发命令）。这从根上消除竞态，不依赖停循环的时机。
  • 案2 当年被否的唯一理由是「UI 要显示送信先番号」——如果这个约束现在变了，案2（StateWinPOSSales.xml:224-226 加一行）反而是最小改动。

所以「是否重新考虑」基本取决于现在 Lanself 带来了什么新约束/新经验。为了给你准确定向，先确认最关键的一点：

Let me reconsider the approaches for the re-do, grounded in code:

The defect (recap): Background while(true) poller in SendMTransactionDialog fires Sales_NotifyCustomer at [:449] unconditionally on OperationState==2; if the SalesTran state has meanwhile moved to WaitingReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction (via 再実行), the state machine rejects the command → dialog freezes. Two sub-defects: (1) no state guard before firing, (2) the loop isn't stopped/restarted around the re-send transition.

Approaches for the re-do:

案1'（移植原方案 = timing-based loop stop/restart） - In BtnRetry_Click [:252-265]: stop the loop (need a real cancellation, since _isChecking flag doesn't break the running thread) before firing ReSend; restart the loop when state returns to SendMTransaction (via CompleteReInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction [:1473]). - Pros: matches what was specified/approved 2 years ago; minimal conceptual change. - Cons: requires introducing a cancellation token / refactor of the bare ThreadPool+Sleep loop (currently has none); still race-sensitive — must guarantee stop completes before the stale loop can observe OperationState==2; the loop lifecycle spread across UpdateDisplay/BtnRetry/event callbacks is the most error-prone part of this dialog. This is likely why it sat un-merged for 2 years.

案3（状态自洽 = guard the fire site）— my recommendation - At [:449] (inside CheckMTransactionOperationState, the OperationState==2 && LastUpdateTerminalNo!=self branch): before AcceptEvent(Sales_NotifyCustomer), check posData.CurrentTran.CurrentState. If the state is not one where Sales_NotifyCustomer is permitted (SendMTransaction / failed / not-found), do NOT fire the command — instead leave the loop running / let the event-driven path (Device_StatusUpdate → CompleteReInquiry → SendMTransaction) bring the state back, after which a subsequent poll will fire the command legally. - Optionally also harden: make the fire idempotent and gated. - Pros: smallest, most robust change; eliminates the race at the root regardless of loop timing; doesn't require refactoring the loop's cancellation; doesn't touch the state machine XML (so 送信先番号 display unaffected); localized to one branch in one file. - Cons: must correctly enumerate the "permitted" states (mirror StateWinPOSSales.xml:212/219); need to ensure that when we suppress the fire, the transaction still converges (the event-driven CompleteReInquiry path must transition to SendMTransaction so the next poll fires legally) — need to verify that path is reliable.

案2（XML allow-list） - Add Sales_NotifyCustomer to [:224-226] waiting-state group. - Pros: one-line change. - Cons: the stated blocker (送信先番号 no longer shown on registration UI) is unchanged per your selection → still rejected. Also it doesn't fix the root (command firing in wrong state); it just makes it legal, which papers over the decoupling.

Hybrid recommendation (案3 + light 案1): Lead with 案3 (state guard at fire site) as the primary, robust fix; optionally pair with a minimal loop-stop in BtnRetry (set a flag the loop checks) as defense-in-depth. Avoid 案2 (constraint unchanged).

When we suppress the fire at [:449] because state is WaitingReInquiry, does the transaction reliably get back to SendMTransaction? The workflow found: Device_StatusUpdate.cs:119-133 routes by state (WaitingReInquiry→CompleteReInquiry), and SalesTran.cs:1473 (CompleteReInquiry, restoreStatus==2) does ChangeState(SendMTransaction). So yes — once the re-inquiry result arrives via the event path, state returns to SendMTransaction, the loop (still running) will on its next poll see OperationState==2 again AND now state==SendMTransaction (permitted) → fires legally. This makes 案3 sound. Good — I can state this with confidence.

But wait — there's a subtlety. The loop at [:449] fires inside CheckMTransactionOperationState, which is called from the polling loop AND from button handlers. When OperationState==2 and we suppress the fire (state not permitted), what does the loop do? Looking at [:196-200]: the loop breaks when CheckMTransactionOperationState != Notyet. When OperationState==2, CheckMTransactionOperationState returns Already ([:454]) — so the loop BREAKS regardless of whether we fired the command. That means if we suppress the fire, the loop exits, and then NOTHING polls anymore until state returns to SendMTransaction and UpdateDisplay spawns a new loop.

So for 案3, when we suppress the fire (state==WaitingReInquiry), we must NOT return Already (which breaks the loop) — we should return Notyet (keep polling) so the loop survives until state returns to SendMTransaction, then fires legally. OR, we break the loop but rely on UpdateDisplay re-spawning it when state becomes SendMTransaction.

Hmm, this is a design detail. UpdateDisplay spawns the loop only when state==SendMTransaction && !_isChecking ([:165, :180]). And _isChecking is set true by the loop ([:195]) and only reset false in BtnClose/BtnRestore ([:240, :280]) — NOT when the loop exits naturally. So if the loop breaks (returns Already), _isChecking stays true, and UpdateDisplay won't re-spawn until something resets _isChecking.

So when CheckMTransactionOperationState returns != Notyet (Already/NotFound), the loop sets _isChecking=false THEN breaks. Good — so _isChecking is reset when the loop exits. That means UpdateDisplay CAN re-spawn the loop later when state==SendMTransaction.

So for 案3, two viable sub-designs: (a) When OperationState==2 but state not permitted: return Notyet from CheckMTransactionOperationState → loop keeps polling (every 500ms) → when event-driven path flips state to SendMTransaction, next poll fires legally. (b) When OperationState==2 but state not permitted: return Already (loop exits, _isChecking reset) → rely on UpdateDisplay to re-spawn when state==SendMTransaction. But between the loop exit and state returning to SendMTransaction, if UpdateDisplay is called with state==SendMTransaction, it re-spawns and fires. This also works but has a gap.

Sub-design (a) is simpler and more robust (no gap). So 案3 = at [:447-450], guard: if current state is not a NotifyCustomer-permitted state, skip the AcceptEvent and return Notyet (keep polling).

But there's another subtlety: CheckMTransactionOperationState sets _isFinished=true at [:434] before [:449]. If we suppress the fire, we should NOT set _isFinished=true (else UpdateDisplay short-circuits at [:124-128] and won't progress). So the guard must also avoid latching _isFinished. This means restructuring the [:430-455] block: only latch _isFinished and break when we actually fire (or when self-restore/NotFound).

Now, which states are "NotifyCustomer-permitted"? From XML: - SalesTran_SendMTransaction (line 212) ✓ - SalesTran_NotFoundInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction, SalesTran_FailedRequestGetMTransaction (line 219) ✓ - NOT the waiting group (line 224).

Actually, more precisely: the loop only runs when state was SendMTransaction. In the bug case, state has moved to WaitingReInquiry (waiting group). So the guard could be simpler: "only fire if state == SendMTransaction" (the state where the loop legitimately runs and NotifyCustomer is allowed). But to be safe and match the XML allow-list, I'd check against the permitted set.

Hmm, but actually — when would state be NotFound/Failed while the loop is running and OperationState==2? The loop spawns in SendMTransaction. If state became NotFound/Failed, UpdateDisplay would take those branches (line 133/151) and not be in SendMTransaction. But the background loop doesn't check SalesTran state — it keeps running. So OperationState==2 could be observed while state is NotFound/Failed/WaitingReInquiry. In NotFound/Failed, NotifyCustomer IS allowed (XML 219), so firing is fine. In WaitingReInquiry, it's not allowed → that's the bug.

So the cleanest guard: fire only if the current state is one where NotifyCustomer is permitted (SendMTransaction, NotFound, Failed). If it's a waiting state (WaitingInquiry/WaitingReInquiry/WaitingRequestGet), suppress + keep polling.

But wait — if state is a waiting state and we keep polling, when does it become legal? Via the event path: Device_StatusUpdate → CompleteReInquiry (SalesTran.cs:1473) → ChangeState(SendMTransaction). So eventually state returns to SendMTransaction → next poll fires legally.

But there's a catch: is the event path guaranteed to fire? The re-send command Sales_ReSendMTransactionManagement calls ReInquiryGetMTransactionResult (async). When that completes, Device_StatusUpdate routes by state (WaitingReInquiry → CompleteReInquiry). CompleteReInquiry with restoreStatus==2 → SendMTransaction. So yes, assuming the re-inquiry completes, state returns to SendMTransaction. Good.

But what if the re-inquiry FAILS or the trade is never accepted? Then state stays WaitingReInquiry, the loop keeps polling OperationState (which is already 2)... and OperationState==2 means it WAS accepted. So if OperationState==2, the trade was accepted, so the re-inquiry should succeed and flip state to SendMTransaction. Convergence is reasonable.

Edge case: OperationState==2 observed, state==WaitingReInquiry, but the re-inquiry result hasn't arrived yet → loop polls every 500ms, each time suppressing fire (state still WaitingReInquiry) → eventually re-inquiry completes → state→SendMTransaction → fire.

There's a possible inefficiency: the loop fires CheckMTransactionOperationState every 500ms, and each call that sees OperationState==2 + non-permitted state must return Notyet (not latch _isFinished, not break). So the design must restructure the OperationState==2 block.

Let me now also reconsider: the timeout timer (_timer). In the bug scenario, BtnRetry stops the timer (line 264). So the timeout won't fire. The loop relies on the event path to converge. If the event path never converges (e.g., re-inquiry hangs), the loop polls forever (every 500ms) — but that's no worse than the current behavior (and the dialog has a Close button the user can press). Actually, in the bug scenario the buttons are disabled (BtnRetry sets SetButtonsEnabled(close.IsEnabled, false, false) at line 261). Hmm. So if convergence fails, the user is stuck with no buttons. Assistant2026-07-03T03:43:43.823Z

已选 案3（状态自洽）。下面是正式设计，请你逐节确认。

设计：案3 — 在发射点对状态自洽

1. 核心思路（一句话）

让 SendMTransactionDialog.xaml.cs:449 这个「会計機取得済 → 发 Sales_NotifyCustomer」的分支，在发命令前先看当前 SalesTran 状态是否允许；不允许（即 bug 窗口：状态已被「再実行」切到再送信等待态）就不发、不锁存、继续轮询，等事件驱动把状态切回合法态后再合法发射。改动只集中在一个方法的一个分支，不碰状态机 XML、不重构循环取消机制。

2. 精确改动点

唯一改动：CheckMTransactionOperationState 的 OperationState == 2 分支（现 :430-455）。概念性 after：

if (result.OperationState == 2)
{
    // 自端末復元 —— 维持原样（latch + 按钮 + Already）
    if (result.LastUpdateTerminalNo == WinPOSFrameworkSettingValues.TerminalNo.Value) { ... return Already; }

    // 会計機で取得済：仅当“当前 SalesTran 状态允许 NotifyCustomer”时才发射
    var s = this._posData?.CurrentTran?.CurrentState;
    bool canNotify = s == SalesTranStates.SendMTransaction
                  || s == SalesTranStates.NotFoundInquiryCanAcceptMTransaction
                  || s == SalesTranStates.FailedRequestGetMTransaction;   // 对照 StateWinPOSSales.xml:212/219 的允许集
    if (!canNotify)
    {
        // bug 窗口：状态处于再送信等待等非许可态。
        // 不发射、不置 _isFinished，返回 Notyet 让轮询循环存活。
        return CheckOperationStateDiv.Notyet;
    }

    lock (_lockObj) { this._isFinished = true; }
    this.Dispatcher.Invoke(() => UIPOSLibrary.AcceptEvent(DeviceIds.TouchDisplay.Id, EventCodes.Sales_NotifyCustomer.Code, null));
    this.StopTimer();
    return CheckOperationStateDiv.Already;
}

关键点：抑制发射时必须返回 Notyet（不是 Already），否则循环会在 :196-200 处 break、且 _isFinished 一旦在 :434 被置 true 会把 UpdateDisplay 在 :124-128 短路——那就又卡死了。所以「不发 + 不锁存 + 返回 Notyet」三者必须同时成立。

3. 正确性论证

  • 正常路径行为不变：正常流程下 OperationState==2 时状态就是 SendMTransaction（循环只在 SendMTransaction 态被起），guard 直接放行 → 与今天完全一致。
  • bug 路径能收敛：抑制后循环每 500ms 继续轮询；事件驱动路径 Device_StatusUpdate.cs:119-133（WaitingReInquiry → CompleteReInquiry）在 SalesTran.cs:1473 把状态切回 SendMTransaction → 下一轮 poll 状态合法 → 合法发射 Sales_NotifyCustomer → Fixed → 对话框关闭。不再卡死。
  • 发射点唯一性：全仓代码里 AcceptEvent(Sales_NotifyCustomer) 只有 :449 这一处（设备层与 CompleteReInquiry 都不直接发该命令），所以这一处 guard 覆盖了全部 bug 路径。

4. 边界与防御

  • _posData 可能为 null → 用 ?. 防御；并需确认 _posData.CurrentTran 是活引用（ChangeState 改的就是它），实现时应验证，否则改成从框架取实时状态。
  • 按钮回调副作用：CheckMTransactionOperationState 也被 BtnClose/Retry/Restore 调用。抑制时返回 Notyet 在 bug 窗口无影响（此时按钮已被 disable），但为彻底安全，可加一个 bool isPolling 入参，仅在轮询上下文抑制、按钮上下文维持原语义。
  • 收敛失败的兜底（可选 defense-in-depth，非案3 核心）：若再問合せ结果迟迟不到、状态回不到 SendMTransaction，循环会持续轮询且按钮 disabled。可考虑在抑制达到一定次数/时间后重新启用「閉じる」或恢复超时定时器——但这属于增强，不阻塞案3 本身。

5. 验证方法（实现后，非我现在执行）

  1. VM 调试复现原 bug 路径（復元拖长 → 送信失敗 → 再実行 → 会計機復元完成）→ 期望：不再冻结，最终正常进入 Fixed 并关闭对话框。
  2. 正常中間取引送信（无再実行）→ 期望：行为与改动前一致（发射、关闭时机不变）。
  3. 自端末復元路径 → 期望：不受影响。
  4. 单测/断言：OperationState==2 + 状态分别为 {SendMTransaction, NotFound, Failed} → 发射；{WaitingInquiry, WaitingReInquiry, WaitingRequestGet} → 抑制且返回 Notyet。

6. 不在案3 范围

  • 不改 StateWinPOSSales.xml（送信先番号显示不受影响）。
  • 不给裸 ThreadPool+Sleep 循环引入 CancellationToken（那是案1' 的事）。
  • 不改三个命令文件、不改设备层。

这个设计你看是否成立？特别是第 2 节的「不发 + 不锁存 + 返回 Notyet」三联、以及第 4 节 isPolling 入参要不要加。

另外提醒：你最初要求只读、不编译/执行——所以我没有把设计写成文件，也没有改任何代码。如果你希望我把这份设计落成一份 spec 文档（比如放到 tasks/5556/ 下，或按 brainstorming 惯例放 docs/superpowers/specs/），告诉我位置我再写；否则就停在设计阶段。

下面是给领导汇报用的总结。我按「问题 → 根因 → 历程与现状 → 结论与建议 → 下一步 → 风险」组织，力求可直接用于汇报；技术细节已收敛到必要程度。

汇报材料：#5556 中間取引送信时画面冻结问题 — 现状与对策总结

一、一句话概述

登録機→会計機发送中間取引时，在特定时序下画面会冻结的缺陷。2 年前已定下对策方向（案1），但始终未合入正式版本；现在需要在新的基线版本上重新处理，建议趁此机会切换到更稳妥、改动更小的「案3」方案。

二、问题现象与影响

  • 现象：操作员在登録機把中間取引发送给会計機时，若会計機侧正在进行数据復元（恢复）处理，画面会卡在「再送信中（重新发送中）」对话框，操作员无法继续、該当收银终端陷入不可操作状态。
  • 发生频率/复现性：实机现场极难复现，在 VM 环境 + 调试器下可稳定复现——属于典型的罕见时序竞态缺陷。
  • 触发条件（时序要求严）：会計機復元耗时较长 → 登録機进入「送信失敗」→ 操作员按下「再実行」→ 恰好此刻会計機復元完成通知到达。三个时机重叠才会发生。

三、根本原因（简明）

登録機侧有一个后台轮询循环，持续检查中間取引是否已被会計機接收，一旦发现「已接收」就发送「送信完成」命令来推进流程。问题在于： 1. 操作员按「再実行」后，交易状态切换到「再送信等待」态，但后台循环并未停止； 2. 循环发现交易已被接收后，在错误的交易状态下发送了「送信完成」命令； 3. 而「再送信等待」态不允许执行该命令，命令被状态机拒绝； 4. 流程因此失去出口，画面永久卡死在「再送信中」。

一句话：「后台循环」与「交易状态」之间缺乏协调，循环不看状态就发命令，是问题的根。

四、处理历程与现状

时间 事项
2023/09 提出问题分析与两个候选方案（案1：调整循环起停时机；案2：放开状态机限制）
2024/08 确认采用案1；交付修复包（基于当时的基线 2024071001）
2024/08 进入受入测试，优先度标为「低」
2025/09 提出「是否需要在新基线模块上重新制作」；优先度由 4 提升至 3
至今 票据状态仍为「新規」，修复从未合入正式代码，当前代码仍是缺陷状态

两点客观判断：① 该修复因优先度低、且原方案改动面较大，长期未排期合入；② 现在基线已更新，原修复包无法直接套用，需要在新基线上重新处理——这正是本次重新评估对策的契机。

五、本次代码分析的结论与建议

经对当前代码的详细核查（已逐处验证），原案1 是「靠时序停掉循环」来规避竞态，本质仍脆弱，且需要给原本没有取消机制的循环补取消逻辑，改动不小、风险不低（这也是它 2 年未合入的原因之一）。

建议在新基线上改用「案3（状态自洽）」： - 做法：在「发命令」这一处加一道判断——只在当前交易状态允许时才发送「送信完成」命令；不允许时就不发、继续等待，等状态自然回到合法态后再合法发送。 - 优点：① 从根上消除竞态，不依赖停循环的精确时机；② 改动面最小（集中在一处）；③ 不改状态机配置，不影响「送信先番号」的 UI 显示（这是当年否决案2 的关键顾虑，得以保留）。 - 可收敛性已验证：等待期间事件驱动机制会把状态切回合法态，流程能正常推进并关闭对话框，不会无限卡死。

六、下一步计划（建议）

  1. 按案3 完成详细设计与评审（含边界处理与回归测试用例）；
  2. 在新基线上实现；
  3. VM 环境复现原 bug 路径验证「不再冻结」，并回归正常中間取引发送、自端末復元等路径；
  4. 受入测试后合入正式版本、关闭票据。

工作量初估：案3 改动集中在一处，预计改修 2~3 人时 + 测试 2~3 人时（较原案1 的改修 4H + 测试 2H 更省；最终以设计评审为准）。

七、风险与需决策事项

  • 历史风险：该缺陷已存在约 3 年（经 1016 天）、状态仍为「新規」，建议明确本次处理的优先度与排期，避免再次搁置。
  • 需确认：① 是否同意采用案3 方向；② 新基线的相关代码是否与本次分析一致（若有差异需重新定位）。
  • 遗留增强（非阻塞）：可考虑在异常长时间未收敛时提供兜底（恢复「关闭」按钮或超时），进一步提升健壮性，可在案3 之后再议。
